            return [...$el.querySelectorAll(selector)]\n
                // All non-disabled elements...\n
                .filter(el => ! el.hasAttribute('disabled'))\n
        },\n
        firstFocusable() { return this.focusables()[0] },\n

        nextFocusable() { return this.focusables()[this.nextFocusableIndex()] || this.firstFocusable() },\n

            let selector = 'a, button, input:not([type=\'hidden\']), textarea, select, details, [tabindex]:not([tabindex=\'-1\'])'\n

        nextFocusable() { return this.focusables()[this.nextFocusableIndex()] || this.firstFocusable() },\n
        prevFocusable() { return this.focusables()[this.prevFocusableIndex()] || this.lastFocusable() },\n
        nextFocusableIndex() { return (this.focusables().indexOf(document.activeElement) + 1) % (this.focusables().length + 1) },\n
        prevFocusableIndex() { return Math.max(0, this.focusables().indexOf(document.activeElement)) -1 },\n
    }"\n

    <div\n
    x-data="{\n
        show: false,\n
        focusables() {\n
            // All focusable element types...\n
            let selector = 'a, button, input:not([type=\'hidden\']), textarea, select, details, [tabindex]:not([tabindex=\'-1\'])'\n
            return [...$el.querySelectorAll(selector)]\n
                // All non-disabled elements...\n

    <div\n
    x-data="{\n
        show: false,\n
        focusables() {\n
            // All focusable element types...\n
            let selector = 'a, button, input:not([type=\'hidden\']), textarea, select, details, [tabindex]:not([tabindex=\'-1\'])'\n
            return [...$el.querySelectorAll(selector)]\n
                // All non-disabled elements...\n
                .filter(el => ! el.hasAttribute('disabled'))\n
        },\n
        firstFocusable() { return this.focusables()[0] },\n
        lastFocusable() { return this.focusables().slice(-1)[0] },\n
        nextFocusable() { return this.focusables()[this.nextFocusableIndex()] || this.firstFocusable() },\n
        prevFocusable() { return this.focusables()[this.prevFocusableIndex()] || this.lastFocusable() },\n
        nextFocusableIndex() { return (this.focusables().indexOf(document.activeElement) + 1) % (this.focusables().length + 1) },\n
        prevFocusableIndex() { return Math.max(0, this.focusables().indexOf(document.activeElement)) -1 },\n
    }"\n
    x-init="$watch('show', value => {\n
        if (value) {\n
            document.body.classList.add('overflow-y-hidden');\n
            \n
        } else {\n
            document.body.classList.remove('overflow-y-hidden');\n
        }\n
    })"\n
    x-on:open-modal.window="$event.detail == 'belanja-pemeliharaan-pc-form' ? show = true : null"\n
    x-on:close-modal.window="$event.detail == 'belanja-pemeliharaan-pc-form' ? show = false : null"\n
    x-on:close.stop="show = false"\n
    x-on:keydown.escape.window="show = false"\n
    x-on:keydown.tab.prevent="$event.shiftKey || nextFocusable().focus()"\n
    x-on:keydown.shift.tab.prevent="prevFocusable().focus()"\n
    x-show="show"\n
    class="fixed inset-0 overflow-y-auto px-4 py-6 sm:px-0 z-50"\n
    style="display: none;"\n

                <div\n
                    x-data\n
                    x-init="\n
                        hitungTotalHarga = () => {\n
                            const v = parseInt(String($wire.volume ?? '').replace(/\D/g, '')) || 0;\n
                            const h = parseInt(String($wire.harga_satuan ?? '').replace(/\D/g, '')) || 0;\n
                            return new Intl.NumberFormat('id-ID').format(v * h);\n

            return [...$el.querySelectorAll(selector)]\n
                // All non-disabled elements...\n
                .filter(el => ! el.hasAttribute('disabled'))\n
        },\n
        firstFocusable() { return this.focusables()[0] },\n
        lastFocusable() { return this.focusables().slice(-1)[0] },\n
        nextFocusable() { return this.focusables()[this.nextFocusableIndex()] || this.firstFocusable() },\n

        nextFocusableIndex() { return (this.focusables().indexOf(document.activeElement) + 1) % (this.focusables().length + 1) },\n

    <div\n
    x-data="{\n
        show: false,\n
        focusables() {\n
            // All focusable element types...\n
            let selector = 'a, button, input:not([type=\'hidden\']), textarea, select, details, [tabindex]:not([tabindex=\'-1\'])'\n
            return [...$el.querySelectorAll(selector)]\n
                // All non-disabled elements...\n
                .filter(el => ! el.hasAttribute('disabled'))\n
        },\n
        firstFocusable() { return this.focusables()[0] },\n
        lastFocusable() { return this.focusables().slice(-1)[0] },\n
        nextFocusable() { return this.focusables()[this.nextFocusableIndex()] || this.firstFocusable() },\n
        prevFocusable() { return this.focusables()[this.prevFocusableIndex()] || this.lastFocusable() },\n
        nextFocusableIndex() { return (this.focusables().indexOf(document.activeElement) + 1) % (this.focusables().length + 1) },\n
        prevFocusableIndex() { return Math.max(0, this.focusables().indexOf(document.activeElement)) -1 },\n
    }"\n
    x-init="$watch('show', value => {\n
        if (value) {\n
            document.body.classList.add('overflow-y-hidden');\n
            \n
        } else {\n
            document.body.classList.remove('overflow-y-hidden');\n
        }\n
    })"\n
    x-on:open-modal.window="$event.detail == 'biaya-pendaftaran-lomba-form' ? show = true : null"\n
    x-on:close-modal.window="$event.detail == 'biaya-pendaftaran-lomba-form' ? show = false : null"\n
    x-on:close.stop="show = false"\n
    x-on:keydown.escape.window="show = false"\n
    x-on:keydown.tab.prevent="$event.shiftKey || nextFocusable().focus()"\n
    x-on:keydown.shift.tab.prevent="prevFocusable().focus()"\n
    x-show="show"\n
    class="fixed inset-0 overflow-y-auto px-4 py-6 sm:px-0 z-50"\n
    style="display: none;"\n
>\n

        x-show="show"\n
        class="fixed inset-0 transform transition-all"\n
        x-on:click="show = false"\n

        x-transition:enter-start="opacity-0"\n
        x-transition:enter-end="opacity-100"\n
        x-transition:leave="ease-in duration-200"\n
        x-transition:leave-start="opacity-100"\n
        x-transition:leave-end="opacity-0"\n
    >\n

        },\n
        firstFocusable() { return this.focusables()[0] },\n
        lastFocusable() { return this.focusables().slice(-1)[0] },\n
        nextFocusable() { return this.focusables()[this.nextFocusableIndex()] || this.firstFocusable() },\n
        prevFocusable() { return this.focusables()[this.prevFocusableIndex()] || this.lastFocusable() },\n
        nextFocusableIndex() { return (this.focusables().indexOf(document.activeElement) + 1) % (this.focusables().length + 1) },\n
        prevFocusableIndex() { return Math.max(0, this.focusables().indexOf(document.activeElement)) -1 },\n

        nextFocusableIndex() { return (this.focusables().indexOf(document.activeElement) + 1) % (this.focusables().length + 1) },\n
        prevFocusableIndex() { return Math.max(0, this.focusables().indexOf(document.activeElement)) -1 },\n

    <div\n
    x-data="{\n
        show: false,\n
        focusables() {\n
            // All focusable element types...\n
            let selector = 'a, button, input:not([type=\'hidden\']), textarea, select, details, [tabindex]:not([tabindex=\'-1\'])'\n
            return [...$el.querySelectorAll(selector)]\n
                // All non-disabled elements...\n
                .filter(el => ! el.hasAttribute('disabled'))\n
        },\n
        firstFocusable() { return this.focusables()[0] },\n
        lastFocusable() { return this.focusables().slice(-1)[0] },\n
        nextFocusable() { return this.focusables()[this.nextFocusableIndex()] || this.firstFocusable() },\n
        prevFocusable() { return this.focusables()[this.prevFocusableIndex()] || this.lastFocusable() },\n
        nextFocusableIndex() { return (this.focusables().indexOf(document.activeElement) + 1) % (this.focusables().length + 1) },\n
        prevFocusableIndex() { return Math.max(0, this.focusables().indexOf(document.activeElement)) -1 },\n
    }"\n
    x-init="$watch('show', value => {\n
        if (value) {\n
            document.body.classList.add('overflow-y-hidden');\n
            \n
        } else {\n
            document.body.classList.remove('overflow-y-hidden');\n
        }\n
    })"\n
    x-on:open-modal.window="$event.detail == 'penerimaan-honor-ptk-form' ? show = true : null"\n
    x-on:close-modal.window="$event.detail == 'penerimaan-honor-ptk-form' ? show = false : null"\n
    x-on:close.stop="show = false"\n
    x-on:keydown.escape.window="show = false"\n
    x-on:keydown.tab.prevent="$event.shiftKey || nextFocusable().focus()"\n
    x-on:keydown.shift.tab.prevent="prevFocusable().focus()"\n
    x-show="show"\n
    class="fixed inset-0 overflow-y-auto px-4 py-6 sm:px-0 z-50"\n
    style="display: none;"\n
>\n

        x-show="show"\n

        x-transition:enter-start="opacity-0"\n
        x-transition:enter-end="opacity-100"\n
        x-transition:leave="ease-in duration-200"\n
        x-transition:leave-start="opacity-100"\n
        x-transition:leave-end="opacity-0"\n
    >\n
